*Hyunho Mo,Djura Smits,Mahlet A. Birhanu,Maarten J. G. Leening,Daniel Bos,Pim van der Harst,Esther E. Bron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种联邦深度学习方法，用于跨异质性人群的隐私保护型心血管疾病风险预测。通过在两个特征不同的人群队列（Lifelines和Rotterdam Study）上训练深度生存模型，联邦学习相比本地训练提升了预测性能（Rotterdam Study的C-statistic从0.728提升至0.739），同时无需共享患者级数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病风险预测模型通常依赖于单机构或集中式数据，跨机构扩展受限于隐私法规和患者数据共享约束。联邦学习虽能解决这一问题，但在医疗场景中面临数据集规模、人群特征和结局定义异质性的挑战。本研究旨在探索联邦深度学习能否在保护隐私的前提下，整合异质性队列以提升心血管疾病风险预测性能。

Method: 采用联邦深度学习方法，整合两个异质性人群队列：Lifelines（148,230名参与者，自我报告结局）和Rotterdam Study（10,155名参与者，临床链接结局）。训练深度生存模型（联邦学习 vs. 本地训练），主要在Rotterdam Study上评估模型性能（因其随访完整）。

Result: 联邦学习训练的深度生存模型在两个队列上均优于本地训练模型。Rotterdam Study：C-statistic从0.728（95% CI: 0.717-0.739）提升至0.739（95% CI: 0.728-0.749）；Lifelines：C-statistic从0.783（95% CI: 0.775-0.791）提升至0.787（95% CI: 0.780-0.792）。

Conclusion: 联邦深度学习能够有效整合异质性队列，在保护个体患者数据隐私的同时提升心血管疾病风险预测性能，为跨机构协作建模提供可行方案。

Abstract: Cardiovascular disease risk prediction models often rely on data from a single institution or centrally pooled datasets. Extending these models across institutions could be limited by privacy regulations and constraints on sharing patient-level data. Federated learning enables collaborative model development without transferring sensitive patient data, but its application in healthcare remains challenging because datasets often differ in size, population characteristics, and outcome definitions. In this study, we present a federated deep learning approach for privacy-preserving cardiovascular disease risk prediction that integrates two population-based cohorts with different characteristics: Lifelines, including 148,230 participants meeting the study inclusion criteria with self-reported outcomes, and the Rotterdam Study, including a smaller cohort of 10,155 participants with digitally linked clinical outcomes. Model performance was primarily evaluated on the Rotterdam Study because of its complete follow-up. Deep survival models trained using federated learning achieved higher predictive performance than models trained locally without federation. For the Rotterdam Study, the C-statistic increased from 0.728 (95% CI: 0.717-0.739) to 0.739 (95% CI: 0.728-0.749). For Lifelines, the C-statistic increased from 0.783 (95% CI: 0.775-0.791) to 0.787 (95% CI: 0.780-0.792). These findings suggest that federated deep learning across heterogeneous cohorts can improve cardiovascular disease risk prediction while preserving the privacy of individual-level patient data.

</details>


### [137] [Steering Neural Network Training through Interpretable Constraints Based on Partial Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08641)
*Yann Claes,Pierre Geurts,Vân Anh Huynh-Thu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于偏依赖的神经网络引导方法，使模型对特定特征的平均响应符合领域知识，在回归问题（如动力系统预测）上比无约束模型表现更好且数据效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性研究多集中于分类问题的解释技术，缺乏对回归问题中解释质量评估和利用先验知识引导模型训练（解释引导学习）的关注。

Method: 提出基于偏依赖（partial dependence）的神经网络引导方法，通过约束模型对特定特征的平均响应，使其与用户提供的功能领域知识对齐。

Result: 在一系列回归问题（包括动力系统预测）上的实验表明，使用该方法训练的模型比无约束模型表现更好、数据效率更高，且其解释与先验知识一致。

Conclusion: 该方法有效实现了解释引导学习在回归问题中的应用，能够使模型既符合领域先验知识，又保持优异的预测性能和数据效率。

Abstract: Over the last few years, there has been an increased interest in making machine learning models more interpretable. Although a great deal of effort goes into developing techniques for interpreting the interactions learned by a given model, fewer studies focus on assessing the quality of such explanations. Even fewer focus on how to adjust the model to produce explanations faithful to prior knowledge, a process known as explanation-guided learning. Furthermore, most approaches in this area focus on classification problems and usually assume prior knowledge about which input features or regions are most important. In this work, we introduce a new approach to steering neural networks based on partial dependence, such that their average response to certain features aligns with specific functional domain knowledge about the problem. We empirically demonstrate on a range of regression problems, including dynamical systems forecasting, that models whose training has been controlled using our method perform better than unconstrained models and are more data-efficient. Moreover, we highlight that interpretations obtained from the former actually align with the user-provided knowledge, whereas those obtained from the latter do not.

</details>


### [138] [BiSCo-LLM: Lookup-Free Binary Spherical Coding for Extreme Low-Bit Large Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08643)
*Yuantian Shao,Peisong Wang,Zhilei Liu,Chuangyi Li,Yuanteng Chen,Pengcheng Xie,Yiwu Yao,Zhihui Wei,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BiSCo-LLM提出了一种无码本的二进制球面编码框架，用于大语言模型的极低比特权重复合，通过球面二值化、残差BSQ阶段和类别级恢复蒸馏，在2比特以下实现高效压缩，且无需存储显式码本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在部署时受限于内存容量、权重带宽和检查点存储；现有低比特压缩方法中，标量/分组量化在接近2比特时表征能力有限，而向量量化虽提供更丰富表征但引入显式码本和额外存储开销。

Method: 1）局部权重块映射到单位超球面并二值化为紧凑球面编码，主载荷为位打包的符号流而非显式VQ质心；2）残差BSQ阶段编码基础球面编解码器的重构误差，提供无码本的显式率-失真路径；3）替换每个Transformer模块类别后进行类别级恢复蒸馏，减少局部权重重构与组装模型行为之间的不匹配；使用8位保护通道路径作为敏感通道的辅助稳定机制，与BSQ载荷分开统计。

Result: 报告的存储预算包括二进制编码、神经解码器、保护通道载荷、LoRA适配器和元数据，实现了极低比特下的高效权重复合。

Conclusion: BiSCo-LLM通过无码本二进制球面编码框架，在避免显式码本存储开销的同时，实现了优于传统标量量化和向量量化的极低比特压缩性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly constrained by memory capacity, weight bandwidth, and checkpoint storage during deployment. Existing low-bit compression methods mainly follow two directions. Scalar or group-wise quantization is simple and compatible with efficient low-precision kernels, but its representation capacity becomes limited when the target budget approaches 2 bits per weight. Vector-quantized weight compression provides a richer block-level representation, but usually introduces explicit codebooks, index lookup, and additional storage accounting. This paper presents BiSCo-LLM, a codebook-free binary spherical coding framework for extreme low-bit LLM weight compression. The core pipeline is built on three components. First, local weight chunks are mapped onto a unit hypersphere and binarized into compact spherical codes, so that the main payload is a bit-packed sign stream rather than explicit VQ centroids. Second, a residual BSQ stage encodes the reconstruction error left by the base spherical codec, providing an explicit rate-distortion path without stored codebooks. Third, category-wise recovery distillation is performed after replacing each Transformer module category, reducing the mismatch between local weight reconstruction and assembled model behavior. A small 8-bit protected-channel path is used as an auxiliary stabilization mechanism for sensitive channels and is counted separately from the BSQ payload. The reported storage budget includes binary codes, neural decoders, protected-channel payloads, LoRA adapters, and metadata.

</details>


### [139] [Multi-Modal, Multi-Environment Machine Teaching for Robust Reward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08647)
*Ali Larian,Qian Lin,Chang Zong Wu,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了逆强化学习中不同反馈模态对奖励函数约束的强度，并提出一种跨多MDP的层次化机器教学算法，通过贪心选择信息丰富的环境和策略性查询低代价反馈，学习对环境动态鲁棒的奖励函数，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IRL的优化教学分析局限于单一环境和仅演示反馈，忽略了异构反馈模态和环境动态如何共同约束跨环境泛化的奖励函数，导致学得的奖励在部署时泛化失败。

Method: 首先理论分析不同反馈模态的约束强度（比较反馈>其他模态）；提出层次化机器教学算法：上层贪心选择能暴露互补奖励约束的多MDP环境，下层在选定环境中策略性查询低代价反馈。

Result: 在相同反馈预算下，该方法相比均匀教学基线实现更低的遗憾值和更强的对未见环境的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 跨环境、多模态的教学对于学习对环境动态鲁棒的奖励函数至关重要。

Abstract: As autonomous agents are increasingly deployed across diverse operational contexts, aligning their behavior with human intent demands reward functions that remain robust to such changes rather than overfitting to any single environment. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) provides a principled way to infer such objectives from human feedback. However, existing analyses of optimal teaching approaches for IRL focus on single-environment, demonstration-only settings, leaving underexplored how heterogeneous feedback modalities and environment dynamics jointly constrain reward functions that generalize across multiple environments. Because demonstrations in one MDP entangle reward information with that environments specific structure, the resulting rewards frequently fail to generalize when the agent is deployed in a new setting. We first analyze how different feedback modalities constrain rewards, showing that, in the unlimited-data regime, comparisons impose strictly stronger global constraints than other modalities. Beyond this theoretical analysis, we introduce a hierarchical machine teaching algorithm for reward learning that operates across multiple MDPs. The algorithm first greedily selects informative environments that expose complementary reward constraints, then strategically queries low-cost feedback within those environments. Empirically, our method achieves substantially lower regret and stronger generalization to held-out environments than uniform teaching baselines under identical feedback budgets, demonstrating the importance of multi-environment, multi-modal teaching for learning dynamics-robust reward functions.

</details>


### [140] [Secure Decentralized Federated Learning via Gossip and Virtual Voting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08651)
*Amirhossein Taherpour,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出gspDAG-FL，一种安全的去中心化联邦学习框架，通过从用于传播模型的同一八卦历史中推导共识，结合拓扑有向无环图和Hashgraph风格虚拟投票，实现模型更新的最终性、拜占庭容错和惰性节点检测，同时避免全局协调开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化联邦学习（DFL）通过点对点八卦传播消除中央服务器，但现有方法缺乏更新最终性证明，且难以抵抗拜占庭或惰性参与者。账本辅助的FL虽提升了可审计性，但区块链、分片或结算委员会会引入与DFL局部性相冲突的全局协调成本。

Method: 节点仅与邻居交换模型负载，全节点收集事件证书和接收者认可的已接受八卦证明，重构紧凑的拓扑有向无环图（Topology DAG），运行Hashgraph风格虚拟投票，生成紧凑的全节点证书。最终性基于唯一的模型来源元组而非参数状态。结合负载验证、已接受证明验证和私有语义审计来提升韧性。

Result: 在MNIST分类和Penn Treebank语言建模任务上，使用公平的留出验证/审计数据，网络规模达N=100，gspDAG-FL在保持接近验证账本FL的学习质量的同时，减少协调瓶颈，提升吞吐量，并在混合拜占庭和惰性参与场景下保持高无效来源检测率。

Conclusion: 形式化定义了对抗设置，证明了控制平面的安全性和条件活性，并给出了时变有效混合下带认证扰动八卦的收敛性保证。

Abstract: Decentralized federated learning (DFL) removes the central server by letting nodes exchange model updates through peer-to-peer gossip, but existing gossip-based methods often lack provenance finality and resilience to Byzantine or lazy participants. Ledger-assisted federated learning (FL) improves auditability, yet blockchains, shards, or settlement committees can reintroduce global coordination costs that conflict with DFL locality. This paper proposes \emph{gspDAG-FL}, a secure DFL framework that derives consensus from the same gossip history used to disseminate models. Nodes exchange model payloads only with neighbors, while full nodes collect event certificates and receiver-endorsed accepted gossip proofs, reconstruct a compact Topology directed acyclic graph (DAG), and run Hashgraph-style virtual voting followed by compact full-node certificates. Finality is over unique model-origin tuples, not identical local parameter states. To improve resilience, gspDAG-FL combines payload validation, accepted-proof validation, and private semantic audit before aggregation. We formalize the adversarial setting, prove safety and conditional liveness of the control plane, and give a convergence guarantee for certified perturbed gossip under time-varying effective mixing. Experiments on MNIST classification and Penn Treebank language modeling, using fair held-out validation/audit data and networks up to \(N=100\), show that gspDAG-FL achieves learning quality close to validation-based ledger FL while reducing coordination bottlenecks, improving throughput, and maintaining high invalid-origin detection under mixed Byzantine and lazy participation.

</details>


### [141] [EdgeRefine: Privacy-Utility Balance for Graphs via Jaccard Sampling under Edge Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08659)
*Wenxiu Ding,Muzhi Liu,Zheng Yan,Mingjun Wang,Yifan Zhao,Qiao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EdgeRefine，一种基于局部差分隐私的自适应边细化框架，通过Jaccard相似度估计边存在概率、按隐私预算ε确定真假边比例并分别采样、以额外采样率k控制总边数，在保护边级差分隐私的同时显著改善了隐私-效用平衡。实验表明，在ε=2.5时，节点分类准确率较最优基线提升17.8%-19.7%，图分类准确率退化仅约5%，且对图重构攻击具有强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络在隐私敏感场景中难以应用，因为图结构可能泄露敏感链接信息。传统方法在邻接矩阵所有元素中注入噪声，但强隐私需要更多噪声，导致效用严重下降，隐私-效用平衡成为实际部署的主要障碍。

Method: EdgeRefine框架包含三个核心步骤：1) 使用Jaccard相似度估计边的存在概率并对边进行排序；2) 根据隐私预算ε确定真实边与虚假边的比例，基于概率排名分别采样；3) 使用额外采样率k控制最终图的边总数，保证图的稀疏性和可靠性。

Result: 在隐私预算ε=2.5下，EdgeRefine在ACM数据集（GAT架构）上节点分类准确率提升17.8%，在Cora数据集（GCN架构）上提升19.7%；图分类准确率退化仅约5%；在图重构攻击下，相对绝对误差在所有隐私预算下均大于1，平均在Cora上为1.962，在AMAP上为1.472。

Conclusion: EdgeRefine通过自适应边细化策略有效改善了边级差分隐私下的隐私-效用平衡，在多种数据集和GNN架构上均显著优于现有隐私保护方法，同时具备对图重构攻击的强大防御能力，为隐私保护图学习提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have shown considerable success in learning from graph-structured data, but their use in privacy-sensitive areas remains difficult because graph structure can leak sensitive link information. To satisfy edge-level differential privacy, a common approach is to inject noise into all elements of the graph's adjacency matrix, thereby obfuscating the existence of any single edge. However, stronger privacy requires more noise, and excessive noise reduces utility, making the privacy-utility balance a major barrier to practical privacy-preserving graph learning.
  To address this issue, we propose EdgeRefine, a local differential privacy framework that improves this trade-off through adaptive edge refinement. EdgeRefine first estimates edge-existence probabilities using Jaccard similarity and ranks edges for noisy edge removal. To ensure the sparsity and reliability of the final graph, it uses the privacy budget $ε$ to determine the ratio of true to false edges, samples them separately based on this probability ranking, and controls the total number of edges with a separate sampling rate $k$. Extensive experiments show that EdgeRefine achieves accuracy comparable to the noise-free baseline and substantially outperforms other privacy-preserving methods across datasets and GNN architectures. Under privacy budget $ε= 2.5$, EdgeRefine improves node classification accuracy over state-of-the-art baselines by 17.8\% on ACM under GAT and 19.7\% on Cora under GCN. In graph classification, it achieves an average accuracy degradation of around 5\% compared to the noise-free baseline. Under graph reconstruction attacks, EdgeRefine maintains relative absolute error levels above 1 across all privacy budgets, averaging 1.962 on Cora and 1.472 on AMAP, indicating strong resilience against privacy leakage.

</details>


### [142] [Resample or Reroute? Budget-Aware Test-Time Model Selection for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08665)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出预算感知的测试时模型选择问题，设计在线重采样或重路由（RoR）分配策略，在给定每查询预算和不完美验证器下，在重采样与重路由之间分配预算以最大化期望正确性。基于11个开源LLM的4个基准实验表明，RoR策略相对于多种基线实现了更优的成本-质量帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM路由系统存在部署路由器与每实例oracle之间的性能差距，且测试时重采样虽能恢复单次提交路由器无法捕获的选择空间，但该保证仅适用于拥有正确标签和无约束预算的理想化oracle，实际部署系统不具备这些条件。尚无工作将重采样已提交模型和重路由到替代模型视为对单查询成本预算的竞争性使用。

Method: 提出预算感知的测试时模型选择框架：给定每查询预算和不完美验证器，在重采样（resampling）与重路由（rerouting）之间分配预算单元以最大化期望正确性。设计基于每单位成本边际正确性估计的在线重采样或重路由（RoR）分配策略，利用选择与采样之间的可恢复性不对称性来支撑策略行为。

Result: 在11个开源LLM的4个不同难度基准上的重放实验表明：RoR策略相对于单路由、单次提交路由器、预算感知Best-of-K、级联和随机分配等基线，实现了更优的成本-质量帕累托前沿；在最异构的基准上增益最大。消融实验显示增益受验证器质量门控，当验证器质量下降时增益缩小。在提供商价格向量和无标签一致性验证器下的鲁棒性重放实验进一步刻画了结论的适用范围。

Conclusion: 预算感知的测试时模型选择是一个重要且未被充分研究的问题；所提RoR策略能有效在重采样和重路由之间分配预算，实现成本与质量的更好权衡；验证器质量是该方法有效性的关键前提。

Abstract: Routing among large language models (LLMs) trades response quality against serving cost, motivated by the reported gap between deployed routers and a per-instance oracle. Recent analysis shows that test-time resampling can recover per-instance selection headroom that no single-commit router captures; however, that guarantee holds only under an idealized oracle equipped with correctness labels and an unconstrained budget, neither of which a deployed system has. To the best of our knowledge, no previous work treats resampling the committed model and rerouting to an alternative model as competing uses of a single per-query cost budget. Therefore, this work formulates budget-aware test-time model selection: given a per-query budget and an imperfect verifier, allocate each unit of budget between resampling and rerouting so that expected correctness is maximized. An online resample-or-reroute (RoR) allocation policy driven by estimated marginal correctness per unit cost is proposed, and its behavior is grounded in the recoverability asymmetry between selection and sampling. Replay experiments on newly regenerated multi-draw correctness tensors from an eleven-model open-weight pool over four benchmarks of differing difficulty show that the proposed RoR policy attains a favorable cost-quality Pareto front relative to single-route, one-commit-router, budget-aware best-of-K, cascade, and random-allocation baselines for the tested pools, with the largest gains on the most heterogeneous benchmark; an ablation further shows the gains are verifier-gated, shrinking as verifier quality degrades, and robustness replays under a provider price vector and a label-free agreement verifier delineate where the conclusions carry over.

</details>


### [143] [A Practical Investigation of Training-free Relaxed Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08690)
*Guoxuan Xia,Luka Ribar,Paul Balanca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了无训练松弛推测解码技术，在统一框架下对比了多种方法，发现松弛方法需要大量能力评估且不适用于轻量级专用起草器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准推测解码是无损的，但近期工作表明放宽这一严格保证可以进一步提升速度、实现能力-速度权衡甚至带来能力提升。本文旨在系统研究无训练的松弛推测解码技术，为从业者提供实用指导。

Method: 将现有的无训练松弛推测解码方法统一到共享框架中，在当代设置下进行基准测试，提取关键发现和经验结论。

Result: 松弛方法需要大量的能力评估（与无损推测解码不同）；许多松弛方法依赖起草器本身是良好的语言模型，因此不适用于轻量级专用多token预测起草器。

Conclusion: 松弛推测解码在实际应用中有明显局限性，尤其是不适合轻量级起草器场景，且评估成本较高。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates sampling from an autoregressive LLM by using a faster auxiliary model to draft tokens which are then verified in parallel by the LLM. Standard speculative decoding is lossless: its rejection and resampling steps exactly preserve the LLM's sampling distribution. Recent work argues that relaxing this strict guarantee can yield further speed-ups, controlled capability-speed trade-offs, or even capability gains. We practically investigate training-free relaxed speculative decoding techniques, unify existing approaches within a shared framework, benchmark them on contemporary settings, and distil takeaways and empirical findings for practitioners. Important takeaways include: relaxation can require considerable capability evaluation unlike lossless speculative decoding, and many relaxed approaches rely on a drafter that is a good language model, making them unsuited for lightweight dedicated multi-token-prediction drafters.

</details>


### [144] [MPFlow: Learning Budgeted Max-Flow Optimization on the Lightning Network with Deep Graph Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08703)
*Harrison Rush,Vincent Davis,Simone Antonelli,Vikash Singh,Jesse Shrader,Emanuele Rossi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究比特币闪电网络中流动性放置问题：给定固定预算，节点应打开哪些通道以最大化路由容量。作者将其建模为图上预算约束的组合优化问题，通过图强化学习选择k条边添加以最大化s-t最大流。轻量级代理结合消息传递策略网络、PPO和动作掩码，并在中心排除课程下训练。在真实闪电网络快照上的实验表明，该方法在最大流目标上持续优于强启发式基线。该代理已部署到生产环境，在30个托管节点上执行了4640次通道打开决策，累计分配267.3 BTC（超1600万美元）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币闪电网络中流动性放置缺乏理论指导，现有方法依赖于启发式或中心节点附着策略，不能有效最大化路由容量。需要一种理论驱动且可实际部署的解决方案。

Method: 将问题形式化为预算约束下的组合优化问题，目标函数为s-t最大流；采用图强化学习方法，结合消息传递策略网络、PPO算法和动作掩码；设计中心排除课程（hub-exclusion curriculum）训练策略，迫使学习容量感知放置而非简单的枢纽附着。

Result: 在多个随机种子和未见图上，该方法在最大流目标上持续优于强启发式基线；已在生产环境中部署，执行4640次通道打开决策，累计分配267.3 BTC（超1600万美元），管理30个节点。

Conclusion: 图强化学习方法能有效解决闪电网络中的流动性放置问题，课程训练策略能学习到更好的容量感知放置策略，并且该方法已成功落地到实际生产环境中，具有显著的经济价值。

Abstract: We address liquidity placement in the Bitcoin Lightning Network (LN): given a fixed budget, which channels should a node open to maximize its routing capacity? We cast this as a budget-constrained combinatorial optimization problem on graphs, selecting $k$ edge additions that maximize $s$--$t$ max-flow, a theory-grounded measure of routing capacity, and solve it with graph reinforcement learning. Our lightweight agent combines a message-passing policy network with proximal policy optimization (PPO) and action masking, and is trained under a hub-exclusion curriculum: the network's top hubs are removed from training subgraphs, forcing the policy to learn capacity-aware placement rather than hub attachment. In extensive experiments on real Lightning Network snapshots, our method consistently outperforms strong heuristic baselines on the max-flow objective across multiple seeds and unseen graphs. The agent has been deployed in production for peer recommendations, executing 4640 channel-open decisions that cumulatively allocate 267.3 BTC over $16 million across 30 managed nodes.

</details>


### [145] [Dimensionality Reduction Meets Network Science: Sensemaking on UMAP's kNN Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08746)
*Duen Horng Chau,Donghao Ren,Fred Hohman,Dominik Moritz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示UMAP内部构建的kNN图在数据探索中的潜力，通过PageRank、k-core分解和聚类系数等图算法能够有效识别代表性数据点、稠密核心区域和紧密邻域，实验证明这些方法在MNIST和Fashion MNIST上具有实用性和竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前UMAP的使用主要聚焦于低维嵌入结果，忽略了其内部构建的kNN图所蕴含的高维流形结构信息，这些信息在UMAP投影过程中会被扭曲。作者旨在挖掘这一被忽视的内部表示的潜力。

Method: 利用UMAP的kNN图，应用标准图算法：(1) PageRank识别代表性数据点，(2) k-core分解检测稠密核心与稀疏外围，(3) 聚类系数发现高相似度的紧密邻域。在MNIST和Fashion MNIST上进行了定量和定性评估。

Result: 图分析方法在代表性点选择（对比k-medoids）和密度聚类（对比HDBSCAN）上具有竞争性或互补性，展示了实用价值。

Conclusion: UMAP的kNN图是一个被低估的丰富数据表示，在其上进行简单的图算法即可获得有意义的分析结果，应成为UMAP标准工作流的一部分。

Abstract: While UMAP is widely used for exploring high-dimensional data, typical workflows focus on its lower-dimensional embedding, largely overlooking the rich k-nearest-neighbor (kNN) graph that UMAP constructs internally. This graph encodes the data manifold in its original high-dimensional space, before the distortion that UMAP's 2D projection introduces. We demonstrate the untapped potential of this internal representation, showing how standard graph algorithms applied to this graph enhance data sensemaking: (1) PageRank identifies representative data points, (2) k-core decomposition reveals dense core regions versus sparse periphery, and (3) clustering coefficient detects tight-knit neighborhoods with highly-similar data points. Through quantitative and qualitative evaluation on MNIST and Fashion MNIST, we show that these graph-based analyses are not only practical but also competitive with or complementary to purpose-built methods (e.g., k-medoids for exemplar selection, HDBSCAN for density-based clustering).

</details>


### [146] [Deep Learning for Joint Narrowband Interference Cancellation and Soft Demodulation in OFDM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08717)
*Emmanouil Kavvousanos,Francky Catthoor,Vassilis Paliouras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种联合深度学习框架（NBI-CNet + LLR-CNet），用于OFDM系统中窄带干扰抑制和鲁棒软解调，消除传统方法存在的错误平层，在严重干扰下仅需0.2-0.5 dB SNR裕度即可达到最优迭代基线性能，计算复杂度降低60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统压缩感知NBI抑制方法存在高顺序延迟，且残留的非高斯结构使LLR不可靠、解码器饱和，导致经典高斯解映射器出现严重错误平层，需要解决这一流水线失配问题。

Method: 提出了由NBI-CNet和LLR-CNet组成的统一深度学习框架。NBI-CNet采用物理信息卷积架构，单次前向传播估计NBI参数并消除多音干扰；LLR-CNet作为结构白化器，将非高斯残留映射为良好校准的软度量。

Result: 计算复杂度相比EOMP-IDS降低60%（N=2048, Q=64）；消除传统基线固有的错误平层；严重干扰（SIR=-10 dB）下在BLER=10^{-4}时仅需0.2-0.5 dB SNR裕度；轻度干扰（SIR=10 dB, Q=12）下编码增益超过3 dB；规避估计误差触发的2×10^{-4}错误平层；尺度不变设计无需重新训练即可泛化至任意FFT尺寸。

Conclusion: 该联合深度学习框架有效解决了OFDM系统中的NBI抑制与软解调流水线失配问题，显著优于传统方法和最优迭代基线，且具备良好的泛化能力和低计算复杂度。

Abstract: Narrowband interference (NBI) severely degrades orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) systems by corrupting subcarriers and rendering classical soft demodulation ineffective. Conventional compressed-sensing (CS) mitigation exhibits high sequential latency and leaves structured, non-Gaussian residuals that cause log-likelihood ratio (LLR) unreliability, decoder saturation, and severe error floors when employing classical Gaussian demappers. We resolve this pipeline mismatch using a unified deep learning framework for joint NBI cancellation and robust soft demodulation. First, NBI-CNet employs a physics-informed convolutional architecture to estimate NBI parameters and remove multi-tone interference in a single forward pass. Without requiring prior knowledge of the active interferer count, NBI-CNet reduces computational complexity by up to 60% ($N{=}2048, Q{=}64$) compared to the state-of-the-art EOMP-IDS algorithm. Second, LLR-CNet acts as a structural whitener by mapping non-Gaussian post-mitigation residuals onto well-calibrated soft metrics. Simulations demonstrate that this joint framework eliminates the error floors inherent to traditional baselines across dense grids. Under severe interference ($\text{SIR}{=}{-}10$ dB), the pipeline operates within a $0.2$ to $0.5$ dB SNR margin of the optimal iterative baseline at a target block error rate (BLER) of $10^{-4}$. Under mild interference ($\text{SIR}{=}10$ dB) with heavy spectral overlap ($Q{=}12$), where classical greedy algorithms erroneously subtract valid data components and corrupt the payload, NBI-CNet avoids signal-peak confusion to deliver a coding gain exceeding $3$ dB. Finally, the architecture circumvents the $2{\times}10^{-4}$ error floor triggered by interferer-estimation errors, while its scale-invariant design enables robust generalization across arbitrary FFT sizes without retraining.

</details>


### [147] [SLORR: Simple and Efficient In-Training Low-Rank Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08754)
*David González-Martínez,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLORR是一个简单、无状态且保持架构不变的低秩正则化框架，通过基于Hoyer稀疏度量和核范数的两种变体，在训练过程中对原始权重矩阵进行正则化，使用GPU友好的近似方法，在ImageNet和LLM预训练中有效提升模型可压缩性，且训练开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练时低秩正则化方法存在需要SVD分解、修改模型架构引入额外参数或依赖有状态缓存等问题，亟需一种简单、无状态、保持架构的解决方案。

Method: 提出SLORR框架，包含基于Hoyer稀疏度量和核范数的两种变体，直接对原始权重矩阵进行正则化，使用GPU友好的近似方法实现前向和反向传播，并提供近似保证。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上（ResNet-50、ViT-B/16、ViT-L/16的短周期继续训练和ResNet-18预训练）引入不到8%训练开销；在135M和560M规模的LLM预训练中，SLORR训练的压缩模型性能显著优于未正则化模型，平均训练开销不到1%。

Conclusion: SLORR能够在不显著增加训练开销的情况下有效诱导模型可压缩性，是一种实用且高效的低秩正则化方法。

Abstract: Low-rank factorization is widely used to compress neural networks, but modern models are often not naturally amenable to aggressive factorization without significant accuracy loss. Existing training-time low-rank regularizers can improve compressibility, but they often require SVDs of large weight matrices, modify the model architecture (introducing additional trainable parameters), or rely on stateful cached quantities. To address these limitations, we introduce SLORR, a simple, stateless, and architecture-preserving framework for in-training low-rank regularization, instantiated with two main variants based on the Hoyer sparsity metric and the nuclear norm. SLORR directly regularizes the original weight matrices using GPU-friendly approximations for the forward and backward passes of the regularizers, for which we provide approximation guarantees. We first evaluate SLORR on ImageNet-1K across short-horizon continued training of ResNet-50, ViT-B/16, and ViT-L/16, and pretraining of ResNet-18, where SLORR induces compressibility while introducing less than 8% training overhead. We further evaluate SLORR-Hoyer in LLM pretraining at 135M and 560M scales: SLORR-trained compressed models preserve performance substantially better than unregularized models while adding less than 1% average training overhead.

</details>


### [148] [Latent Memory Palace: Reasoning for Control as Autoregressive Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08724)
*Chuning Zhu,Eva Xu,Jose Barreiros,Krishnan Srinivasan,Paarth Shah,Abhishek Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Memory Palace (LMP)方法，通过自回归潜空间中的迭代检索实现控制策略的类人推理，并推导了潜空间强化学习技术以优化变分下界。LMP-π在仿真和真实场景中表现优异，LMP-tok作为变长动作分词器提升了自回归策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类决策具有高度灵活性，能根据情况选择即时行动或深思熟虑。语言模型已展现出类似的适应性"推理"能力，但将这种能力迁移到连续控制策略中一直具有挑战性，因为直接在语言空间中推理缺乏空间理解和精确运动所需的粒度。

Method: 将推理形式化为具有自回归潜分布的变分推断，推导潜空间强化学习技术以可优化地训练其变分下界，构建Latent Memory Palace (LMP)框架。

Result: LMP-π在仿真和真实世界领域均取得强经验性能，展现出可解释的、自适应的测试时计算分配。LMP-tok作为变长动作分词器显著提升了自回归策略的性能。

Conclusion: 通过变分推断视角为潜空间推理用于控制提供了新视角，证明了自回归潜空间可以像"记忆宫殿"一样组织信息，实现迭代和自适应的检索过程。

Abstract: Human decision-making is highly flexible -- some actions are taken immediately; others require longer deliberation. Language models have exhibited a similar capacity for adaptive "reasoning." However, transferring this capability to continuous control policies has been challenging, as directly reasoning in language space may lack the granularity for spatial understanding and precise motions. In this work, we show that reasoning for control policies can emerge by organizing information in an autoregressive latent space reminiscent of a memory palace, where retrieval is iterative and adaptive. Our method, Latent Memory Palace (LMP), formulates reasoning as variational inference with an autoregressive latent distribution. We derive a latent-space reinforcement learning technique to tractably optimize its variational lower bound. The resulting policy, LMP-$π$, achieves strong empirical performance in simulation and real-world domains while exhibiting interpretable, adaptive allocation of test-time compute. We further show that the same framework yields a variable-length action tokenizer, LMP-$\texttt{tok}$, which significantly improves the performance of downstream autoregressive policies. Together, these results present a new perspective on latent reasoning for control through the lens of variational inference.

</details>


### [149] [Super Weights in LLMs and the Failure of Selective Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08733)
*Shreyas Subramanian,Adewale Akinfaderin,Akarsha Sehwag*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型中的“超级权重”（Super Weights）虽然对模型性能至关重要，但单独训练这些参数会导致精度降至随机猜测水平，而通过LoRA等结构化低秩更新整个层才有效，证明参数重要性不等于可训练性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有工作识别出“超级权重”（单个移除即大幅降低模型性能的参数），但这些参数是否真正可训练、单独微调是否有效尚未被探究，作者希望验证超级权重的可训练性。

Method: 在OLMo-1B和OLMo-7B上进行实验：(1) 单独训练超级权重（100~8,192个参数）；(2) 扩展到超级权重的局部邻域（最多36K参数）；(3) 对比随机位置训练；(4) 使用LoRA在完整层上进行低秩更新；(5) 在LoRA更新中约束仅保留超级权重坐标位置的更新进行消融研究。

Result: 单独训练超级权重精度降至随机猜测水平；扩展到局部邻域无改善；随机位置训练反而优于基线；完整LoRA（仅0.16%参数）成功；约束LoRA更新至超级权重坐标的结果与完整LoRA无统计学差异。

Conclusion: 参数重要性不等于参数可训练性，有效的微调依赖于对整个层的结构化分解，而非针对个体重要参数的孤立训练。

Abstract: Recent work identified Super Weights, individual parameters whose removal degrades model performance by orders of magnitude. We show that this degradation due to pruning Super Weights does not universally apply to all LLMs. Furthermore, if these parameters are so important, Super Weight-aware training should be effective. We show the opposite. Training Super Weights in isolation (100 to 8,192 parameters) drops accuracy to random-guessing levels on both OLMo-1B and OLMo-7B, and expanding to local neighborhoods of up to 36K parameters provides no improvement. The failure is specific to Super Weight coordinates: training an equal number of randomly chosen positions in the same down_proj layers instead improves over the baseline, so the collapse comes from targeting Super Weights, not from sparsity itself. Vanilla LoRA, updating every position in attention weight matrices through low-rank structure, succeeds with only 0.16% of parameters, and applying the same low-rank update to down_proj succeeds as well. A 10-seed ablation confirms that constraining LoRA updates at positions corresponding to Super Weight coordinates yields statistically indistinguishable results. These findings establish that parameter importance does not imply parameter trainability in isolation, and that effective fine-tuning relies on structured decompositions over entire layers rather than targeting individually important weights.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [BACH: A Bayesian Admixture of Contrastive Heads for Multi-Interest Two-Tower Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08107)
*Quoc Phong Nguyen,Paul Albert,Long Vuong,Vuong Le,Julien Monteil*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: BACH提出了一种贝叶斯混合对比头方法，通过变分推断对每个用户建模为多个兴趣头的软混合，解决了硬路由训练中的头塌陷问题，并在多个大规模推荐基准上提升了检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双塔检索器将每个用户压缩为单一向量，难以覆盖用户的多样化兴趣；现有多头兴趣模型采用硬路由训练导致头利用率低（路由塌陷），且无法提供每个兴趣对用户重要性的估计。

Method: 提出BACH（贝叶斯对比头混合模型），将多头双塔检索建模为每个用户关于多个兴趣头的软混合分布，通过变分推断进行拟合。软混合确保每个头都被训练（缓解塌陷），并产生每个用户的兴趣权重，该权重可在服务阶段复用。还提出共享全局码本变体，支持预计算检索。

Result: 在MovieLens-20M、Taobao和Netflix三个大规模基准上，BACH在所有头数量下均优于硬路由多头和单向量基线；进一步发现使用每个候选的最佳头进行评分（与服务一致）优于目标路由训练，BACH在此基础上进一步提升。

Conclusion: BACH通过软混合机制有效缓解了多头检索中的路由塌陷问题，并为每个用户提供了可解释的兴趣权重，在多个推荐数据集上取得了优越的检索性能。

Abstract: Two-tower retrievers compress each user into a single embedding, limiting their ability to serve diverse interests. Multi-interest models give each user several heads scored by a maximum inner product, but their hard-routing training under-utilizes heads (routing collapse) and gives no per-user estimate of how much each interest matters for serving. We present \textbf{BACH} (\emph{Bayesian Admixture of Contrastive Heads}), which casts multi-interest two-tower retrieval as a per-user mixture over the heads, fit by variational inference. The soft mixture trains every head (mitigating collapse), produces a per-user weighting of the interests that is reused at serving, and admits a shared global-codebook variant with precomputable retrieval. On three large-scale benchmarks, MovieLens-20M, Taobao, and Netflix, BACH improves top-of-ranking retrieval over hard-routing multi-interest and single-vector baselines at every head count; we further find that scoring every candidate by its best head, consistent with serving, outperforms the usual target-routed training, and that BACH improves further still.

</details>


### [151] [DaV-Gen: End-to-End Generative Retrieval via Draft-and-Verify](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08365)
*Meng Zhao,Chunmei Liu,Qinyong Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DaV-Gen，一种受投机解码启发的“草稿-验证”统一框架，通过单一模型内的对比损失和融合损失联合训练，实现搜索与推荐系统的高效端到端生成，兼顾草稿阶段的速度与验证阶段的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多阶段级联架构（MCA）在不同阶段存在优化目标不一致的问题，导致早期错误被传播或放大，影响最终结果质量；而现有的端到端生成模型因解码器自回归过程导致在线推理效率低下。

Method: 提出DaV-Gen框架，将生成任务重构为“草稿-验证”两个协同操作：1）训练阶段，通过对比损失优化嵌入空间以支持高效向量草稿，同时通过融合损失（生成似然+向量相似度）实现精细验证；2）推理阶段，先执行高效向量草稿生成候选集，再使用融合评分函数进行验证。

Result: 在统一的端到端架构中同时实现了稀疏草稿的速度和先进生成模型的精度，有效弥补了传统MCA与纯自回归生成模型之间的差距。

Conclusion: DaV-Gen通过单一模型内的草稿-验证机制，从根本上重构了搜索与推荐的范式，为工业级信息检索系统提供了兼顾效率与效果的统一解决方案。

Abstract: Mainstream industrial information retrieval systems (e.g., search and recommendation) are usually built upon Multi-Stage Cascade Architectures (MCAs), which balance effectiveness and efficiency through a coarse-to-fine ``retrieval-ranking'' pipeline. However, the optimization objectives across different stages are substantially inconsistent, propagating or even amplifying the early-stage errors that ultimately degrade the quality of final results. While emerging end-to-end generative models offer a potential solution by unifying the pipeline, their online serving performance is severely hindered by the auto-regressive process inherited from the standard decoder-only structure. To bridge this gap, we introduce \textbf{DaV-Gen}, a novel unified solution designed to fundamentally refactor the paradigm for both search and recommendation via a ``Draft-and-Verify'' mechanism. Inspired by the process used by speculative decoding, our framework redesigns the generation task into two synergistic operations within a single model. During training, the model is concurrently optimized for both candidate drafting and fine-grained verification. This is achieved by a composite loss function that jointly trains the model on two distinct but related objectives: 1) a contrastive loss that structures the embedding space for efficient drafting, and 2) a fusion loss that combines generative likelihood with vector similarity to produce a superior verification score. This integrated training strategy equips the model with dual capabilities. At inference time, it first performs highly efficient vector-based drafting to generate a candidate set, and then verifies these candidates using the more powerful fused scoring function, thereby achieving both the speed of sparse drafting and the precision of advanced generative models within a unified, end-to-end architecture.

</details>


### [152] [H3D: Benchmarking Unsupervised Text Hashing for Fine-Grained Document Deduplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08382)
*Qianren Mao,Jiaxun Lyu,Junnan Liu,Zhijun Chen,Jingzheng Li,Hanwen Hao,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: H3D是一个用于细粒度科学文档去重的无监督文本哈希基准，系统评估了非学习方法（MinHash、SimHash等）和基于BGE嵌入的语义敏感方法，在CSFCube和RELISH两个数据集上从排序质量、效率和鲁棒性三个维度进行对比，发现词法与结构哈希在近重复匹配中更有优势，而语义敏感表示在内容改写场景下相似性保持更好但计算成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏在统一协议下对细粒度科学文档进行哈希流水线比较，需要建立一个标准化的基准来系统评估不同哈希方法。

Method: 构建H3D基准，包含五种无监督非学习哈希方法（MinHash、SimHash、Winnowing、FuzzyHash、FlyHash）和两种基于冻结BGE嵌入的语义敏感方法（BGE-BIHash和BGE-LSHash），在CSFCube（细粒度面级分析）和RELISH（大规模分割级评估）两个数据集上，采用MAP、NDCG@20、效率和鲁棒性指标进行联合评估。

Result: 词法和结构哈希在近重复匹配中表现出竞争力，语义敏感表示在内容改写场景下能更好保持相似性但计算成本更高，不同相似性度量在特定哈希表示下存在排序等价性。

Conclusion: 不同哈希方法存在一致的性能权衡，非学习方法适合近重复检测，语义敏感方法适合内容改写场景，研究还提高了方法比较的可解释性和可复现性。

Abstract: Document hashing provides compact representations for efficient similarity search and document deduplication, but existing studies rarely compare hashing pipelines under a unified protocol for fine-grained scientific documents. H3D is an unsupervised text hashing benchmark for fine-grained document deduplication. It evaluates representative unsupervised non-learning hashing approaches (MinHash, SimHash, Winnowing, FuzzyHash, FlyHash) together with semantic-sensitive methods built from frozen BGE embeddings and two quantization strategies (BGE-BIHash and BGE-LSHash). The non-learning methods generate hash fingerprints through manually designed mathematical rules without training or labeled similarity pairs, which distinguishes them from neural semantic hashing models. We benchmark all methods on CSFCube and RELISH, two datasets that provide complementary evaluation settings: facet-level analysis for scientific-document similarity and larger-scale split-level evaluation for biomedical similarity search. H3D jointly reports ranking quality (MAP, NDCG@20), efficiency, and robustness under controlled text compression. The results show a consistent trade-off: lexical and structural fingerprints are competitive for near-duplicate matching, while semantic-sensitive representations better preserve similarity under content rewriting, at higher computational cost. We further analyze when different similarity measures become rank-equivalent for specific hash representations, improving the interpretability and reproducibility of method comparisons.

</details>


### [153] [Conversational Retrieval and On-the-Fly Knowledge Modeling of Historical Penitentiary Repression Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08459)
*Paula Font Solà,Adrià Molina Rodríguez,Josep Lladós*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向历史数字图书馆的文档分析系统，通过图结构存储专家知识和文档检索事实，支持语言模型在持续交互中访问已建模知识，从而实现跨文档的长程依赖查询、链接发现和专家知识整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RAG的数字图书馆系统在处理整体性文档集合理解和动态专家知识整合方面存在局限，难以支持复杂跨文档查询和隐含知识的利用。

Method: 构建图结构索引作为语言模型的可访问记忆，存储专家档案管理员产生的事实和文档检索推理的事实；通过持续专业交互，系统可从原始文档和已建模知识中检索信息。

Result: 系统能够支持涉及跨文档长程依赖的复杂查询、链接发现以及专家知识整合，生成更丰富和全面的信息。

Conclusion: 该方法通过图结构知识建模增强了数字图书馆的文档分析能力，实现了动态知识整合与复杂推理。

Abstract: Recent developments in digital libraries increasingly favor conversational and natural language access to information through Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). Although these approaches are effective for extractive tasks grounded in individual records, they remain limited in their ability to interpret document collections holistically and to incorporate expert knowledge dynamically. In this article, we present a document analysis system designed for the management of historical digital libraries that supports on-the-fly knowledge modeling. The system is equipped with the capability to store facts produced either by expert archivists or derived from document retrieval processes within a graph-based structure. Through continuous professional interaction, the system can retrieve information not only from primary sources such as documents, but also from previously modeled knowledge, with the graph-based index acting as a memory for the language model to access. This enables increasingly complex queries involving long-term dependencies across documents, link discovery, and the integration of expert knowledge that may not be explicitly present in the original sources. As a result, the proposed approach facilitates the generation of richer and more comprehensive information.

</details>


### [154] [Log-Insight: Automating Microservice Incident Diagnosis via Neuro-Symbolic Log Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08529)
*Carlos Garcia-Hernandez,Aymane Abdali,Guangyu Wu,Mingxue Wang,Fei Shen,Zhaoyu Pang,Yanbin Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Log-Insight是一个部署在华为生产环境的自动化事故诊断系统，通过六阶段流水线将百万级原始日志事件缩减1000-7000倍，保留统计显著的故障信号，在11个生产事故上实现MRR=0.790，90%以上运行中正确根因位于Top-3假设内，延迟小于一分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模微服务系统中，单次30分钟事故窗口可产生超过200万行日志（约12亿字符），远超LLM上下文窗口限制。现有基于模板的解析器缺乏语义异常推理能力，深度学习检测器输出黑盒二进制信号，LLM管道在原始遥测数据上遭遇上下文溢出和领域幻觉，无法有效进行根因分析。

Method: 核心设计原则是自动化SRE的手动排障工作流：六阶段符号化流水线复制熟练SRE的结构化调查过程——采样、模式理解、聚类、统计异常排序，将百万级原始事件压缩1000-7000倍后，向LLM提供紧凑的预排序证据档案，由其综合生成假设报告。

Result: 在11个历史生产事故（110次运行，SRE验证的ground truth）上，Log-Insight达到MRR=0.790，超过90%运行中正确根因位于Top-3假设内，延迟低于一分钟。法医证据部分（精确日志模板和偏斜统计）被操作员认定为关键采纳因素。

Conclusion: Log-Insight通过符号化预处理器弥补了现有方法的空白，将LLM角色从处理原始数据转变为分析预压缩证据，实现了高效准确的根因分析。报告了系统性故障模式、主动缓解措施和开放研究方向，并将系统角色从不透明预言机转变为调查助手。

Abstract: Diagnosing production incidents in large-scale microservice systems is time-critical for Site Reliability Engineers (SREs). A single 30-minute incident window in our deployment can generate over two million log lines--approximately 1.2 billion characters, far exceeding standard LLM context windows--making direct LLM-based Root Cause Analysis (RCA) infeasible. Existing approaches leave gaps: template-based parsers lack semantic anomaly reasoning, deep-learning detectors emit black-box binary signals, and LLM pipelines suffer context overflow and domain hallucination on raw telemetry.
  We present Log-Insight, an automated incident-diagnosis system deployed in production at Huawei. The core design principle automates the SRE's manual triage workflow: symbolic stages replicate the structured investigation a skilled SRE would perform--sampling, schema understanding, pattern clustering, and statistical anomaly ranking. This hands the LLM a compact, pre-ranked evidence dossier to synthesise into a hypothesis report. Our six-stage pipeline reduces millions of raw events by 1,000-7,000x while preserving statistically significant failure signals.
  Evaluated on 11 historical production incidents (110 runs, SRE-validated ground truth), Log-Insight achieves MRR = 0.790, returning the correct root cause within the top-3 hypotheses in over 90% of runs in under a minute of latency. We report systematic failure modes, active mitigations, and open research directions. The Forensic Evidence section--listing exact log templates and skew statistics--was consistently identified by operators as a key adoption factor, shifting the system's perceived role from opaque oracle to investigative assistant.

</details>


### [155] [Improving Ad-hoc Search Effectiveness for Conversational Information Retrieval via Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08540)
*Ahmed Rayane Kebir,Jose G. Moreno,Lynda Tamine*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出模型融合（Model Soup和Slerp）作为无需训练的策略，将对话检索器与通用检索器融合，使单一模型同时适用于常规搜索和对话检索，避免传统微调带来的灾难性遗忘，在零样本条件下NDCG@3提升高达15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对话信息检索依赖对常规检索器进行微调或多任务学习，但这种方法成本高（需要重新训练模型），且存在灾难性遗忘问题——模型会丧失原有的常规检索能力。本文旨在填补这一空白，提出无需训练的模型融合策略。

Method: 采用线性（Model Soup）和非线性（Slerp）的参数级模型融合策略，将常规检索器与对话检索器的参数进行融合，无需额外微调，使单一模型同时适用于常规搜索和对话检索两种场景。

Result: 模型融合显著增强了对话检索器的常规搜索能力，同时提升了跨任务数据集的泛化能力，在零样本条件下NDCG@3提升高达15%。

Conclusion: 模型融合是一种有效的训练无关策略，能够解决对话检索中灾难性遗忘问题，兼顾常规检索与对话检索性能，且无需重新训练模型。

Abstract: Conversational information retrieval is challenging since it requires the consideration of the conversation history which potentially gives rise to topic shifts and coreference resolution across previous turns. To address these challenges, previous work mainly rely on traditional fine-tuning of ad-hoc retrievers on conversational datasets or extrapolates their generalizability through multi-tasking. However, this mainstream approach is costly - since it requires model re-training - and exhibits catastrophic forgetting, where the model loses its foundational ad-hoc retrieval performance. In this paper, we fill this gap by introducing model merging as a training-free strategy enabling the design of a single retrieval model that operates across both ad-hoc and conversational settings with no additional fine-tuning. We conduct experiments using linear and non-linear parameter-wise merging strategies - namely Model Soup and Slerp - on standard ad-hoc search and conversational retrieval datasets. Our results demonstrate that model merging significantly enhances the ad-hoc search capabilities of conversational retrievers while improving generalizability across task-specific datasets, achieving up to 15% higher NDCG@3 under zero-shot conditions.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [REFORGE: A Method for Benchmarking LLMs' Reverse Engineering Capabilities in Decompiled Binary Function Naming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07738)
*Nicolas Koller,Andreas u. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Reforge，一个构建二进制分析基准真相的流程管道，揭示编译器优化导致的对齐不确定性使LLM评估产生幸存者偏差，并推动不确定性感知的基准测试实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM辅助二进制分析基准将函数级真实标签的构建视为已解决的预处理步骤，报告准确率时未说明有多少函数可被可靠评估。作者认为公平评估的主要障碍不是模型能力，而是编译器优化下二进制到源代码对齐的可靠性问题。

Method: 提出Reforge流程管道，从C源代码开始，经过编译、DWARF和语法提取、对齐、反编译，构建函数级真实标签。将对齐不确定性操作化为八门置信漏斗和三层次分层机制。在受控微基准上评估不同优化级别下的高置信度产出，并对7个当代LLM进行函数命名评估。

Result: 高置信度产出从87.2%（无优化）下降到65.9%（高优化）；未配对比较通过幸存者偏差夸大优化导致的性能衰减；7个LLM在函数命名任务上的评估展示了该基底的效用。

Conclusion: 二进制逆向分析的基准测试需要不确定性感知的评估实践，Reforge为这一方向提供了可行的技术方案和实证依据。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to reverse-engineering tasks, and recent threat-intelligence reporting shows them operating inside live offensive-security workflows. Claims about their capability, however, outpace our ability to measure it. Existing benchmarks for LLM-assisted binary analysis treat the construction of function-level ground truth as a solved pre-processing step and report accuracy without disclosing how many functions were reliably evaluable. We argue that the principal obstacle to fair evaluation is not model capability but the reliability of binary-to-source alignment under compiler optimization. This paper presents Reforge, a provenance-tracked pipeline that constructs function-level ground truth from C source through compilation, DWARF and syntactic extraction, alignment, and decompilation, and that operationalizes alignment uncertainty as an eight-gate confidence funnel with three-tier stratification. On a controlled micro-benchmark, high-confidence yield falls from 87.2% to 65.9% across optimization levels, and unpaired comparisons overstate optimization-induced performance decay through survivorship bias. A proof-of-concept evaluation of seven contemporary LLMs on function naming demonstrates the substrate and motivates uncertainty-aware benchmarking practice

</details>


### [157] [PERFOPT-Bench: Evaluating Coding Agents on Software Performance Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07744)
*Yingyun Cui,Yi Xie,Piaohong Wang,Jiawei Ma,Bo Liu,Liangliang Cao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出PERFOPT-Bench基准，用于评估代码智能体在性能优化任务中的全流程能力，包括性能分析、瓶颈诊断、代码修改和正确性验证。实验发现优化性能与工作负载高度相关，而非仅由模型决定；原始加速比作为评分指标存在安全风险，因为部分大幅加速源于基准特定的捷径利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体基准主要衡量智能体生成功能正确补丁的能力，但生产软件还需要在真实执行目标上实现可测量的加速。性能优化作为一个独特的智能体任务，需要智能体具备性能分析、跨层瓶颈诊断、在不破坏正确性的前提下编辑代码以及验证加速的可重复性等能力，现有基准缺乏对此完整性能工程循环的评估。

Method: 构建PERFOPT-Bench基准，每个任务提供一个正确但有意次优的代码库，要求智能体改进目标性能指标；评分机制包含隐藏的正确性测试、验证加速比测量和轨迹级审计。在7个长周期优化任务上评估了7种不同LLM和智能体框架的组合。

Result: 优化性能取决于工作负载而非模型身份；没有单一智能体栈全面占优，改变智能体框架会显著改变同一LLM在不同任务上的加速比分布。原始加速比作为基准分数不安全，因为部分大幅加速来自基准特定的捷径利用。探索性的接力实验表明，从外部化优化摘要重新启动可以在初始会话停止后恢复额外的优化空间。

Conclusion: 性能优化作为智能体任务需要更全面的评估框架，PERFOPT-Bench填补了这一空白。原始加速比指标存在安全风险，需要结合正确性验证和轨迹审计来确保评估的可靠性。接力式优化策略有望进一步提升智能体的优化能力。

Abstract: Coding-agent benchmarks have largely measured whether agents can produce functionally correct patches, but production software also demands measurable speedups on real execution targets. Performance optimization is a distinct agentic task: agents must profile executions, diagnose cross-layer bottlenecks, edit code without breaking correctness, and verify that gains are reproducible rather than measurement artifacts. We introduce PERFOPT-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating this full performance-engineering loop. Each task provides a correct but deliberately suboptimal codebase and asks the agent to improve a target performance metric; scoring requires hidden correctness tests, verified-speedup measurement, and trajectory-level audit. We evaluate 7 agent stacks with different LLMs and agent frameworks on 7 long-horizon optimization tasks. The results show that optimization performance is workload-dependent rather than determined by model identity alone: no single stack dominates, and changing the agent framework can materially change the same LLM's per-task speedup profile. We further find that raw speedup is unsafe as a benchmark score, since some large gains arise from benchmark-specific shortcut exploitation; an exploratory relay pilot suggests that restarting from an externalized optimization summary can recover additional headroom after an initial session stops. The benchmark and our evaluation are available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Dataset-D3CC.

</details>


### [158] [Functional and Secure Code Generation with Task Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07881)
*Felix Wang,Anudeep Das,Mei Nagappan,N. Asokan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SecVecCoder方法，利用任务向量算术同时提升代码LLM生成代码的功能性与安全性，无需后处理即可生成可信代码。在六种编码LLM上的实验表明，该方法将可信代码生成率提升2.1-36.0个百分点，对未见CWE类型的改进高达39.1个百分点，且解码延迟仅增加0.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成LLM在生成功能正确且无安全漏洞的代码方面存在困难。先前工作要么使用不同数据集分别评估代码功能与安全性，要么在生成后才发现漏洞，缺乏同时保证功能与安全性的方法。

Method: 借鉴文本对齐任务中的任务向量算术思想，对LLM权重进行线性操作，使模型同时具备功能正确与安全可靠的代码生成能力，无需后处理调整。

Result: 在六个编码LLM（来自三个模型家族）的CodeGuard+基准测试上，可信代码完成率提升2.1-36.0个百分点，未见过的CWE类型改进高达39.1个百分点。解码延迟仅为基础模型的0.6%。

Conclusion: SecVecCoder通过任务向量算术有效提升了代码LLM生成可信代码的能力，而无需特定解码方法，计算开销极小。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for code generation, but they struggle to generate functional code free of security vulnerabilities. Prior work to improve the secure code generation abilities of such coding LLMs has largely focused on evaluating code functionality and security separately using different datasets, or focused on finding vulnerabilities post-generation. At the same time, the text-generation domain has seen significant work on alignment techniques, where models are tuned such that their outputs exhibit certain qualities (e.g., helpfulness, harmlessness). Of particular interest is task-vector arithmetic, where linear operations on LLM weights can be used to arbitrarily enhance alignment while incurring only minimal computational overhead. We develop a novel method, SecVecCoder, leveraging task vectors to produce trustworthy code that is simultaneously functional and secure without the need for post-generation adjustment. Across six coding LLMs from three families on the CodeGuard+ benchmark, SecVecCoder improves the rate of trustworthy code completions by 2.1-36.0 percentage points over the base model, with improvements on unseen CWE types reaching up to 39.1 percentage points. Since the effectiveness of the coding LLM relies only on changing the model weights, SecVecCoder requires no method-specific decoding and hence achieves a decoding latency within 0.6% of the base model's, on average.

</details>


### [159] [Bug Report Specification Refinement with Trajectory Guidance for Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07882)
*S M Farah Al Fahim,Md Nakhla Rafi,Md Ahasanuzzaman,Zeyang Ma,Dong Jae Kim,Shaowei Wang,Tse-Hsun,Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TrajSpec是一种轨迹引导的缺陷报告细化方法，通过收集预修复仓库上的未验证轨迹，提取三层证据表示（高层解释、诊断发现、具体观察），并经过仓库评审生成细化报告。在SWE-Bench Lite的300个实例上，TrajSpec将GPT-5-mini的Pass@1从41.00%提升至59.67%，MiniMax M2.5从54.67%提升至64.33%，消融实验表明仓库评审和层级证据表示对性能至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺陷报告通常只描述观测到的失败现象，缺少故障诱导行为、行为需求和实现范围等修复相关信息，导致自动程序修复代理可能检查无关代码、推断错误需求或生成仅解决表面症状而未恢复仓库预期行为的补丁。

Method: TrajSpec首先运行轨迹收集代理获取预修复仓库上的未验证轨迹，从中提取轨迹派生的证据；将证据组织为三层表示：高层问题解释、支持该解释的诊断发现、具体仓库观察；生成草稿细化报告后，应用基于仓库的评审来移除无依据声明、修正不确定声明并添加仓库支持的细节。

Result: 在SWE-Bench Lite的300个实例上，TrajSpec将GPT-5-mini的Pass@1从41.00%提升至59.67%，MiniMax M2.5从54.67%提升至64.33%。在100个实例的分层样本上，Agentless从41.00%提升至71.00%，AutoCodeRover从47.00%提升至72.00%。消融实验显示移除仓库评审或层级证据表示后Pass@1分别降至48.00%和47.67%。

Conclusion: TrajSpec通过提供可操作的、仓库支持的上下文，能够一致地提升多种自动程序修复代理的修复性能，证明了轨迹引导的缺陷报告细化方法的有效性。

Abstract: Bug reports serve as task specifications for repository-level automated program repair (APR) agents, but they often describe only the observed failure and omit repair-relevant information such as the failure-inducing behavior, behavioral requirement, and implementation scope. As a result, a repair agent may inspect irrelevant code, infer an incorrect requirement, or generate a patch that addresses the reported symptom without restoring the intended repository behavior. We present TrajSpec, a trajectory-guided approach for repository-supported bug report specification refinement. Given an original report and a pre-fix repository, TrajSpec runs a trajectory-collection agent and uses the resulting unverified trajectory as a source of trajectory-derived specification evidence. It organizes this evidence into a three-level representation consisting of a high-level interpretation of the issue, diagnostic findings supporting that interpretation, and concrete repository observations. TrajSpec then generates a draft refined report and applies repository-based review to remove unsupported claims, revise uncertain claims, and add repository-supported details. We evaluate TrajSpec on all 300 SWE-Bench Lite instances using Mini-SWE-Agent V2. TrajSpec's refined reports improve Pass@1 from 41.00% to 59.67% with GPT-5-mini and from 54.67% to 64.33% with MiniMax M2.5. On a stratified sample of 100 instances, TrajSpec's refined reports also improve Pass@1 from 41.00% to 71.00% with Agentless and from 47.00% to 72.00% with AutoCodeRover. Ablation results show that removing repository-based review or the hierarchical evidence representation reduces Pass@1 from 59.67% to 48.00% and 47.67%, respectively. Overall, TrajSpec provides actionable repository-supported context that consistently improves repair performance.

</details>


### [160] [The Memory Wall of Green Software: Empirical Energy Evaluation of Memento Design Pattern](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07944)
*Imane Jriri,Tarik Houichime,Younes El Amrani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了Memento设计模式的能耗特性，对比了基线、经典快照和差分增量策略。通过RAPL接口测量10MB-200MB状态体积下的能耗，发现差分策略在中等规模下最多节能65.8%，但在200MB时遭遇"内存墙"，GC抖动和非线性功耗尖峰完全抵消算法优化优势。研究为绿色架构设计提供了基于证据的启发式规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绿色软件工程日益成熟，能效已成为关键的非功能性需求。然而软件设计模式虽确保结构完整性，其固有的抽象层会施加隐性的"代谢成本"，这一成本在设计阶段常被忽视。本文旨在揭示这种成本的具体表现。

Method: 选取Memento设计模式为研究对象，设置三种对比策略：无抽象基线、经典全快照(Classic full-snapshot)和差分增量编码(Differential delta-encoding)。利用Intel RAPL接口进行高保真硬件遥测，测量状态体积从10MB到200MB规模化下的能量耗散。

Result: 差分策略在中规模状态下最多可降低65.8%能耗；但在200MB饱和点时遭遇"内存墙"——严重的GC抖动和非线性功耗尖峰完全抹消了算法优化带来的能效收益。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了关键架构权衡：设计模式的抽象优化在不同规模下表现迥异，需警惕极端规模下的"内存墙"效应。研究提供了基于实证的启发式规则，帮助架构师在结构设计质量与绿色IT可持续发展之间取得平衡。

Abstract: As Green Software Engineering matures, energy efficiency has transitioned into a mission-critical non-functional requirement. While software design patterns ensure structural integrity, their inherent abstraction layers impose an implicit "metabolic cost" that often remains obscured during the design phase. This paper empirically investigates the energy dynamics of the Memento design pattern, contrasting a direct, unabstracted baseline against Classic full-snapshot and Differential delta-encoding strategies. Leveraging the RAPL interface for high-fidelity hardware telemetry, we quantify energy dissipation across state volumes scaling from 10 MB to 200 MB. Our empirical results expose a critical architectural trade-off: the Differential strategy minimizes memory traffic, yielding a maximum energy reduction of 65.8% for mid-scale states, but collides with a catastrophic "memory wall" at 200 MB. At this saturation point, algorithmic optimizations are completely neutralized by severe GC thrashing and non-linear power spikes. We synthesize these findings into evidence-based heuristics, providing architects with a robust framework to reconcile structural design quality with sustainable Green IT imperatives.

</details>


### [161] [DeepSWE: Measuring Frontier Coding Agents on Original, Long-Horizon Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07946)
*Wenqi Huang,Charley Lee,Leonard Tng,Serena Ge*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DeepSWE是一个包含113个原创、长周期软件工程任务的基准测试，用于评估编码智能体。它通过从头编写任务、不贡献回上游仓库、使用手写验证器等方式，解决了现有基准测试（如SWE-bench）中存在的预训练数据污染和测试用例不准确两个核心问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体基准测试（如SWE-bench）存在两个严重问题：（1）从公开GitHub仓库挖掘的合并修复可能已被模型在预训练阶段见过，导致高分反映的是记忆而非问题解决能力；（2）任务评分依赖伴随合并修复的测试用例，这些测试是为确认特定修复而写的，可能误判正确替代方案或放过不完整方案。

Method: DeepSWE的113个任务从头编写，覆盖91个活跃开源仓库和5种编程语言，不向上游贡献参考解决方案，确保不进入模型训练抓取的公开记录；每个任务使用手写验证器检查请求的功能，接受任何能提供该功能的实现；使用独立LLM法官重新评审评分运行以评估准确性。

Result: 独立LLM法官与DeepSWE验证器的分歧率仅为1.4%，远低于SWE-Bench Pro继承测试的32.4%；DeepSWE提示长度约为SWE-Bench Pro的一半，但描述任务的参考解决方案涉及5.5倍更多的代码；该基准测试能在更宽的分数带上区分前沿智能体，而其他排行榜上这些智能体得分往往聚在一起。

Conclusion: DeepSWE通过避免数据污染和测试不准确问题，提供了更可靠的编码智能体评估基准，能更有效区分前沿模型的真实问题解决能力。

Abstract: DeepSWE is a benchmark of 113 original, long-horizon software engineering tasks for evaluating coding agents. Most public agentic coding benchmarks follow SWE-bench in mining merged fixes from public GitHub repositories, which creates two problems: the fixes and their discussion were likely seen during pretraining, so a high score can reflect recall rather than problem-solving; and each task is graded by the tests that shipped with its merged fix, which were written to confirm one specific fix rather than grade an arbitrary solution, so they can fail a correct alternative or pass an incomplete one. DeepSWE avoids both. Its tasks are written from scratch across 91 active open-source repositories and five languages and are never contributed back upstream, so their reference solutions stay out of the public record that model training scrapes; and each task is graded by a hand-written verifier that checks the requested functionality and accepts any implementation that provides it. When an independent LLM judge re-reviews graded runs, it disagrees with DeepSWE's verifier about an order of magnitude less often than with SWE-Bench Pro's inherited tests (1.4% versus 32.4%). Despite being about half the length of SWE-Bench Pro's prompts, DeepSWE's prompts describe tasks whose reference solutions touch 5.5x more code, and the benchmark separates frontier agents across a wider score band than the leaderboards on which they otherwise cluster. We release the benchmark, its verifiers, and the full record of evaluation trajectories.

</details>


### [162] [3100 Opinions on Code Review in an AI World: Building Causal Theory from Practitioner Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07980)
*Shyam Agarwal,Courtney Miller,Christian Kästner,Bogdan Vasilescu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模灰色文献分析构建了一个因果理论模型，阐明AI编码代理如何改变代码审查实践：审查成为控制点，团队的专业知识和审查流程结构决定AI对软件的影响方向，而非AI本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践者对AI编码代理如何影响代码审查存在尖锐分歧（审查是否成为瓶颈、人工审查是否仍必要、是否侵蚀了曾经建立的理解），而现有的仓库挖掘研究虽能测量表面趋势却无法解释底层机制，且趋势本身不稳定。

Method: 收集38,709篇灰色文献（工程博客和Reddit话题），筛选出与代码审查实质相关的内容，对分层随机抽样的3,100篇进行LLM辅助编码，构建包含26个构念和67个关系（64个有向、3个有争议）的因果模型。

Result: 该因果理论的核心主张是：审查是决定编码代理对软件影响的控制点，AI本身并不固定影响的正负符号——团队通过其人类专家的专业知识以及如何组织审查流程来设定影响方向。

Conclusion: 该理论将竞争性立场显式化，将"AI正在改变代码审查"转化为带有命名构念和调节变量的可证伪命题，并提供了可扩展的LLM辅助灰色文献理论构建方法作为软件工程研究的模板。

Abstract: Coding agents now author entire pull requests, and practitioners sharply disagree about what this does to code review: whether it becomes the bottleneck, whether human review is still necessary, and whether it quietly erodes the understanding that it once built. Repository-mining studies measure surface trends but seldom explain the mechanisms beneath them, and the trends themselves prove unstable. A motivating observational analysis of public GITHUB activity finds that agent-authored pull requests are reviewed less often, merged several times faster, and discussed less than human-authored ones, yet the direction of these trends flips under different but equally defensible analysis choices, so the traces establish what is changing without explaining why. To recover the mechanisms, we synthesize practitioner discourse at scale into an explanatory theory: we collect 38,709 grey-literature documents (engineering blogs and Reddit threads), filter to those substantively about code review, and code a stratified random sample of 3,100 with an LLM-assisted pipeline, from which we build a causal model of 26 constructs and 67 relationships (64 directed, 3 contested). Its organizing claim is that review is the control point through which a coding agent's effect on software is decided, and that AI does not fix the sign of that effect: the team sets it, through the expertise its humans bring and how it structures the review process. The theory makes the competing positions explicit and turns "AI is changing code review" into falsifiable propositions with named constructs and moderators. As a secondary contribution, we offer the underlying LLM-assisted, grey-literature theory-building method as a scalable template for software-engineering research, with a public implementation.

</details>


### [163] [On the Correctness of Software Merge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07987)
*Akira Mori,Masatomo Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于范畴论推出概念的语法合并工具，要求合并结果既可解析又具普遍性，并通过大规模实验验证了其相比现有工具（如Git合并工具）能更正确地处理代码合并场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的三路合并工具缺乏严谨的正确性定义，其结果常被不加审视地接受，导致合并错误未被及时发现。

Method: 提出基于范畴论推出概念的合并正确性准则（可解析性与普遍性），并实现相应的结构化合并工具，在大规模Java项目上进行了实验验证。

Result: 在43,774个文件合并场景中，现有工具存在大量错误合并结果，而所提工具未报告错误；与2,582个人工合并和2,459个涉及21种重构类型的合并场景比较，揭示了结构化合并的优势与当前局限性。

Conclusion: 所提出的正确性准则为开发更可靠和更有原则的合并工具提供了基础，强调明确正确性标准的重要性。

Abstract: Three-way merge tools play crucial roles in modern software development, where a developer forks a branch to make local modifications and requests it to be merged into the main branch via a "pull request." Despite its importance, the task has traditionally been defined in an intuitive manner, and the results of merge tools are often accepted without scrutiny. In this paper, we present a new structural merge tool in comparison with existing tools based on the syntactic criteria we propose for evaluating the merge results. We require the merge result to be both parsable and universal. Being parsable means that the result is syntactically valid according to the grammar of the programming language. Being universal means that the result incorporates all and only the edit operations occurring in each branch while ensuring that edits common to both branches are applied only once. This requirement can be precisely defined using the notion of pushouts in category theory. In a large-scale experiment involving 43,774 file merge scenarios from 76 open-source Java projects, we found a number of incorrect results reported by existing tools such as the Git companion merge tool, whereas our tool reports none. We further compared d3j's results with 2,582 developer-resolved merges and with 2,459 merge scenarios involving 21 refactoring types. These experiments revealed both the strengths and current limitations of structural merge, and underscore the importance of clear correctness criteria. We expect that the proposed criterion will provide a foundation for developing more reliable and principled merge tools.

</details>


### [164] [Aleena: Alignment Agent for Research Software Engineering Collaborations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08043)
*Kshitij Dani,Cordero Core,Landung Setiawan,Carlos Garcia Jurado Suarez,Anshul Tambay,Vani Mandava,Anant Mittal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Aleena，一个开源的生命周期对齐代理，利用GitHub作为协作界面，将多模态利益相关者交互转化为结构化项目记录，以解决研究软件工程中由于跨多种沟通渠道（Slack、会议、PR等）导致的决策连续性丢失和心智模型分歧问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究软件协作中决策分散在Slack、会议、GitHub Issues/PR等多个渠道，导致领域研究者与研究软件工程师之间的项目意图、所有权和科学假设等心智模型产生分歧，决策原始依据丢失。

Method: 提出Aleena系统——一个开源的生命周期对齐代理，以GitHub为统一协作面，利用Agentic AI将多模态利益相关者交互转换为结构化项目记录，用于暴露风险、追踪未解决问题并保持决策连续性。

Result: 基于大学研究软件工程中心的经验，展示了Aleena的系统设计、原型实现和说明性生命周期场景。

Conclusion: 研究软件工程中的对齐是一个连续的生命周期问题，Agentic AI可以在不取代人类决策的前提下支持利益相关者对齐和项目状态跟踪，Aleena为此提供了可行的系统解决方案。

Abstract: Research software collaborations span meetings, informal chats, pull requests, and GitHub issues. A decision surfaced in a Slack thread, refined in a meeting, and implemented in a pull request can lose its original rationale across these artifacts, leaving domain researchers and research software engineers with divergent mental models of project intent, ownership, and scientific assumptions. We argue that alignment in research software engineering is a continuous lifecycle problem, and that agentic AI can support stakeholder alignment and project-state tracking without replacing human decision-making. We present Aleena, an open-source lifecycle alignment agent that uses GitHub as a shared collaboration surface, transforming multi-modal stakeholder interactions into structured project records that surface risks, track open questions, and preserve decision continuity. Grounded in university-based research software engineering center experiences, this paper presents the motivating problem, system design, prototype, and illustrative lifecycle scenarios for Aleena.

</details>


### [165] [TTHE: Test-Time Harness Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08124)
*Jun Nie,Yonggang Zhang,Jun Song,Qianshu Cai,Dahai Yu,Yike Guo,Xinmei Tian,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出测试时框架演化（TTHE），在评估过程中通过演化可执行控制程序（而非更新模型权重）来优化LLM智能体的行为，无需标签或任务特定监督。实验表明，TTHE在多个任务上持续改进固定基线框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在部署前搜索固定智能体工作流并在测试时冻结，无法适应测试分布、失败模式或工具交互的变化。作者希望在测试时利用无标签执行轨迹优化智能体框架，实现自适应改进。

Method: TTHE将可执行框架视为测试时自适应状态，维护候选框架种群，通过智能体提议者基于执行轨迹推理进行改进，再由裁判从执行派生的代理信号中确定改进版本，所有角色均基于同一个冻结LLM，仅通过改变外围程序实现自适应。

Result: 在文本到SQL、竞赛编程、软件工程、数据科学编码和智能体工具使用等任务上，TTHE改进了固定的ReAct风格基线框架，产生了持久、可检查的改进，而非预搜索工作流或逐查询重试。

Conclusion: 测试时自适应可被重构为对可执行控制程序的演化，执行派生代理信号的可靠性是实现鲁棒无监督智能体改进的核心挑战。

Abstract: The behavior of an LLM agent is determined not only by the underlying model, but also by its harness: the executable program that constructs context, invokes tools, verifies intermediate results, and recovers from failures. Existing approaches optimize such harnesses before deployment, searching training or development data for a fixed agent workflow that is then frozen at test time. This limits adaptation when the test distribution, failure modes, or tool interactions differ from those seen during development. We ask whether the harness can instead be optimized during evaluation itself, using only the unlabeled execution traces the agent produces on the test inputs. We introduce Test-Time Harness Evolution (TTHE), which treats the executable harness as the state of test-time adaptation. During evaluation, TTHE maintains a population of candidate harnesses and refines them through an agentic proposer that reasons over their execution traces, without gold labels or task-specific supervision; a judge then commits an improved harness from execution-derived proxy signals, and the selected program persists to govern subsequent inputs. Crucially, TTHE does not update model weights, require gold labels, or train a separate adaptation model: solver, proposers, and judge are different roles and harnesses around the same frozen LLM, so all adaptation occurs through changes to the surrounding program. Across text-to-SQL, competitive programming, software engineering, data-science coding, and agentic tool-use tasks, TTHE improves fixed ReAct-style baseline harnesses, yielding persistent, inspectable improvements rather than a pre-searched workflow or per-query retries. These results recast test-time adaptation for LLM agents as evolution over executable control programs and identify execution-derived proxy reliability as a central challenge for robust unsupervised agent improvement.

</details>


### [166] [ProjAgent: Procedural Similarity Retrieval for Repository-Level Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08691)
*QiHong Chen,Aaron Imani,Iftekhar Ahmed*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ProjAgent是一个面向仓库级代码生成的系统，通过引入过程相似性作为显式检索信号，将目标函数分解为中间推理步骤，结合智能体工作流检索具有相似过程行为的仓库函数，并与传统语义检索融合构建更丰富的代码生成上下文，同时通过静态分析反馈循环迭代修复生成代码，在REPOCOD上达到41.14%的Pass@1，优于现有检索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级代码生成的检索方法主要依赖词汇、结构或语义相似性，忽略了仓库中实现相似过程逻辑但标识符或应用领域不同的函数，导致检索上下文不充分。

Method: ProjAgent将目标函数分解为中间推理步骤，通过智能体工作流检索具有相似过程行为的仓库函数，将过程上下文与常规语义检索融合构建更丰富的仓库上下文，并采用保守的静态分析反馈循环利用编译器和静态分析反馈迭代修复生成代码。

Result: 在REPOCOD基准上，ProjAgent达到41.14%的Pass@1，优于现有的基于检索的基线方法。

Conclusion: 过程相似性是仓库级代码生成中一个有效且此前未被探索的检索维度。

Abstract: Repository-level code generation requires implementing target functions while accounting for complex cross-file dependencies and project-specific conventions. Existing retrieval methods predominantly rely on lexical, structural, or semantic similarity, often overlooking repository functions that implement similar procedural logic despite differing in identifiers or application domains. We propose ProjAgent, a repository-level code generation system that introduces procedural similarity as an explicit retrieval signal. ProjAgent decomposes the target function into intermediate reasoning steps and employs an agentic workflow to retrieve repository functions that exhibit similar procedural behavior at each step. The retrieved procedural context is integrated with conventional semantic retrieval to construct a richer repository context for code generation. ProjAgent further incorporates a conservative static-analysis feedback loop that iteratively repairs generated code using compiler and static-analysis feedback. Evaluated on REPOCOD, ProjAgent achieves 41.14% Pass@1, outperforming existing retrieval-based baselines. These results demonstrate that procedural similarity is an effective and previously unexplored retrieval dimension for repository-level code generation.

</details>
